TL;DR: 首次成功应用深度强化学习方法（改进的JAMPR模型）解决带容量和时间窗约束的取送货问题，在小中规模问题上实现快速最优解，在大规模问题（>200）上实现快速次优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全球城市人口增长的背景下，车辆取送货最优路径构建是一项极具前景的任务。虽然小规模问题可通过经典方法解决，但在现实世界约束（如容量和时间窗约束）下，针对中大规模问题的快速（或实时）路径优化器仍然是一个高度挑战性的任务。

Method: 首次成功应用深度强化学习方法，具体采用改进的JAMPR模型，来解决带容量和时间窗约束的取送货问题（CPDPTW）。

Result: 获得了一个鲁棒的模型，能够针对小中规模问题给出快速最优解，针对大规模问题（>200）给出快速次优解。

Conclusion: 证明了深度强化学习方法在解决复杂约束下的取送货路径优化问题上的有效性，特别是填补了中大规模问题实时优化的技术空白。

Abstract: The task of constructing vehicles optimal routes for pickup and delivery of goods is one of most promising tasks in the context of global urban population growth. Although this kind of problems with small size can be solved by various classical approaches, a fast (or realtime) route optimizer under the constraints of the real world (such as capacity and time windows constraints) for medium-large size problems still remains a highly challenging task. In this work we, for the first time, successfully applied a deep Reinforcing Learning approach (modified JAMPR model) to solve Pickup and Delivery problem with Capacity and Time Window constraints (CPDPTW). We obtained a robust model that gives a fast optimal solution for problems of small and medium size, and gives fast suboptimal solution for problems of larger (> 200) size.

</details>


### [46] [Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14177)
*Xuanmian He,Can Li,Wanjing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SGSAN模型，通过显式学习静态有向依赖图和基于InfoNCE的软耦合机制，在保持最优预测精度的同时提供内在可解释性，解决了时空图神经网络在交通流预测中缺乏透明度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度时空图神经网络虽然在网络级交通流预测中表现优异，但由于缺乏透明度，在安全关键的城市系统中部署受限。现有的后验诊断方法难以处理虚假相关性，无法揭示交通动力学的内在决策机制，导致可解释性不足和操作可信度有限。

Method: 提出结构引导的时空注意力图神经网络(SGSAN)：1)显式学习静态有向依赖图(DDG)以识别交通状态的宏观传播路径；2)引入基于InfoNCE的软耦合机制，将动态时空注意力锚定到结构先验；3)开发解耦的两阶段优化框架，解决结构发现与预测误差最小化之间的冲突。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SGSAN实现了最先进的预测精度，同时提供了与交通网络物理逻辑有机一致的内置可解释性，注意力推理与宏观依赖相一致并防止过度依赖瞬时局部噪声。

Conclusion: SGSAN成功解决了传统无约束自适应图方法的局限性，通过将注意力推理与宏观依赖对齐，实现了高预测精度与可解释性的统一，为安全关键的城市交通系统部署提供了可信的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep spatiotemporal models integrating graph convolutions and attention mechanisms have demonstrated excellent performance in network-level traffic flow prediction, owing to their exceptional ability to capture complex spatiotemporal dependencies. Despite their predictive success, deployment of such models in safety-critical urban systems remains constrained by their inherent lack of transparency. Existing post-hoc diagnostic methods often struggle with spurious correlations and fail to unveil the intrinsic decision-making mechanisms governing traffic dynamics, resulting in suboptimal interpretability and limited operational trustworthiness. To address these challenges, this paper proposes the Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network (SGSAN). Departing from traditional architectures that rely on unconstrained adaptive graphs, SGSAN explicitly learns a static Directed Dependency Graph (DDG) to identify the invariant macroscopic propagation paths of traffic states. We further introduce an InfoNCE-based soft-coupling mechanism that anchors the model's dynamic spatiotemporal attention to this structural prior, offering a mechanistic account of the model's decision-making process while ensuring robust forecasting by aligning attention-based reasoning with identified macroscopic dependencies and preventing over-reliance on ephemeral local noise. Furthermore, a decoupled two-stage optimization framework is developed to resolve the fundamental conflict between structural discovery and predictive error minimization. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that SGSAN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while providing built-in interpretability that organically aligns with the physical logic of traffic networks.

</details>


### [47] [Revisiting Energy-based Tabular Anomaly Detection: Energy and Reconstruction are Complementary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14186)
*Junichiro Niimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了经典深度玻尔兹曼机(DBM)在表格异常检测中具有竞争力，其平均场能量与自编码器融合后显著提升性能，为EBMs在该领域的应用提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格异常检测主要被基于密度代理的方法(如Isolation Forest, OCSVM)、基于重建的方法(如Autoencoders)和现代非参数评分器(如COPOD)所主导，但都仅间接近似正常分布；显式基于能量的模型在此领域几乎缺失。受深度学习中EBMs复兴的启发，研究重新审视经典深度玻尔兹曼机在该任务中的潜力。

Method: 采用双隐藏层深度玻尔兹曼机(DBM)，将其平均场能量作为异常评分。在两个不同领域的表格数据基准(UCI Bank Marketing和NSL-KDD)上，与八个经典和现代基线方法进行对比，使用20个随机种子进行评估。通过秩融合将DBM能量与自编码器结合。

Result: DBM平均场能量在Bank Marketing上匹配最强基线(自编码器)，在NSL-KDD上统计显著超越它，并显著优于其他七个基线。DBM与自编码器融合后在两个数据集上均获得统计显著提升(前者AUROC+0.014，后者+0.002)；而其他非DBM基模型与自编码器配对时均无法改善甚至显著降低集成性能。

Conclusion: 经典基于能量的模型(以DBM为代表)应纳入表格异常检测工具箱，作为当前实践中占主导地位的重建类方法的非冗余补充视角。

Abstract: Tabular anomaly detection is dominated by classical density-proxy methods (Isolation Forest, OCSVM, LOF), reconstruction-based detectors (Autoencoders, VAEs), and modern non-parametric scorers (COPOD, ECOD, Deep SVDD), all of which approximate the inlier distribution only indirectly; explicit energy-based models are largely absent. Motivated by the recent revival of EBMs in deep learning (e.g., Energy-Based Transformers, JEPA), we revisit the classical Deep Boltzmann Machine (DBM) for this task and hypothesize that its mean-field energy combines more effectively with a reconstruction-based score than same-lineage pairs do. We evaluate a two-hidden-layer DBM on two tabular benchmarks spanning distinct domains (UCI Bank Marketing and NSL-KDD) against eight classical and modern baselines across twenty random seeds. The DBM mean-field energy matches the strongest baseline (the Autoencoder) on Bank Marketing and statistically beats it on NSL-KDD, while significantly outperforming the remaining seven on both datasets. When fused with the Autoencoder via rank fusion, the DBM energy yields a statistically significant improvement on both datasets (AUROC=+0.014, p<0.01 on Bank Marketing; +0.002, p<0.001 on NSL-KDD); every non-DBM-derived base model instead fails to improve or significantly degrades the AE-paired ensemble. Our position is that classical EBMs, exemplified by the DBM, deserve a place in the tabular anomaly detection toolbox as a non-redundant complementary view to the reconstruction-based scores that dominate current practice.

</details>


### [48] [KV Cache Compression Through the Lens of Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14191)
*Hannah Laus,Claudio Mayrink Verdun,Hao Wang,Flavio du Pin Calmon,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力感知变换编码（AATC）方法，通过理论分析和优化比特分配，在约5.8倍压缩率下实现近乎无损的KV缓存压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 键值（KV）缓存是长上下文推理中的主要内存瓶颈。现有量化方法通过降低精度数据类型表示KV缓存，并设计量化方案以最小化缓存本身的重建误差，但未考虑该误差如何通过注意力机制传播。

Method: 本文首先在白噪声量化模型下证明期望注意力感知失真可分解为加性的键和值贡献，并在令牌和通道上因子化。基于变换编码和逆注水等信号处理和率失真理论的经典工具，提出注意力感知变换编码（AATC）方法，在校准集上分配比特以最小化注意力感知失真。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct模型上，于LongBench、RULER、GSM8K、MMLU-Pro和MATH-500等多个基准测试中，该方法在约5.8倍压缩率下实现近乎无损的准确率，而所有基线方法在至少某些设置下都出现性能下降。

Conclusion: 通过理论推导提出的注意力感知变换编码方法，相比现有量化方法，在保持准确率的同时实现了高效的KV缓存压缩，为解决长上下文推理中的内存瓶颈提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache stores information from past tokens and is a major memory bottleneck in long-context inference. Existing quantization methods address this bottleneck by representing the KV cache uniformly with lower-precision data types and designing quantization schemes to minimize reconstruction error in the cache itself, without accounting for how that error propagates through attention mechanisms. We prove that, under a white-noise quantization model, the expected attention-aware distortion decomposes into additive key and value contributions that factor across tokens and channels. Building on transform coding and reverse water-filling, which are classical tools from signal processing and rate-distortion theory, we introduce Attention-Aware Transform Coding (AATC), which allocates bits over a calibration set to minimize attention-aware distortion. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, evaluated across LongBench, RULER, GSM8K, MMLU-Pro, and MATH-500, our method achieves near-lossless accuracy at approximately $5.8\times$ compression, whereas each baseline degrades in at least some settings.

</details>


### [49] [MINT: A Universal Zero-Shot Predictor for Transaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14198)
*Parameswaran Kamalaruban,Viktor Drobnyi,Maeve Madigan,Julia Rozanova,David Sutton,Stuart Burrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MINT框架，通过轻量级嵌入注入、交易-语言对齐和指令微调，将预训练的交易序列编码器与解码器LLM连接，实现了在交易数据上的高效零样本预测，在性能和效率上均优于文本序列化基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的支付基础模型无法灵活地在新颖的下游预测任务上进行零样本推理，限制了其适应性和实用性；而现有的基于LLM的零样本预测方法往往无法充分利用交易数据中的预测信号，且依赖于昂贵的文本序列化或扩展性差的任务特定架构。

Method: 提出MINT（Multimodal Instruction Network for Transactions）框架，通过轻量级嵌入注入、交易-语言对齐和指令微调三个关键组件，将预训练的交易序列编码器连接到仅解码器的LLM，使紧凑的交易嵌入能够被LLM理解并用于推理。

Result: MINT在分布内和分布外问题上均达到了最先进的预测性问答性能；同时相比文本序列化基线，显著减少了输入标记、延迟和内存消耗。全面分析表明，紧凑的交易嵌入在多模态推理和零样本预测任务中优于文本序列化。

Conclusion: 紧凑的交易嵌入是比文本序列化更优的交易表示方法，能够更好地支持多模态推理和零样本预测任务，MINT成功实现了高效且准确的交易数据零样本预测。

Abstract: Banks analyse sequential financial transaction data to perform many tasks, including fraud prevention, credit risk assessment and offer personalization. To improve the predictive accuracy of these tasks, Payments Foundation Models encode transaction sequence data as rich contextual embeddings, which can then be provided to task-specific models as features. However, these Foundation Models are not designed for flexible zero-shot reasoning across novel downstream prediction tasks, limiting their adaptability and utility. Existing LLM-based approaches to zero-shot prediction often fail to fully exploit the predictive signal within transaction data, while relying on costly text serialization or task-specific architectures that scale poorly. To address these limitations, we present the Multimodal Instruction Network for Transactions (MINT), a framework that connects a pretrained transaction sequence encoder to a decoder-only LLM through lightweight embedding injection, transaction-language alignment, and instruction tuning. We find that MINT achieves state-of-the-art predictive question-answering performance in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution questions, while substantially reducing input tokens, latency, and memory consumption compared to text-serialization baselines. Through comprehensive analyses of representations, alignment strategies, training data, and history length, we establish that compact transaction embeddings are a superior approach to transaction representation than text serialization for multimodal reasoning and zero-shot prediction tasks.

</details>


### [50] [Adaptive Protection for Evolutionary Feature Construction in Symbolic Regression with Application to Credit Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14209)
*Hengzhe Zhang,Qi Chen,Bing Xue,Lean Yu,Wolfgang Banzhaf,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于特征重要性的自适应保护机制，在进化特征构造中选择性保留重要构建特征，显著提升符号回归及信用分类任务的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化特征构造方法缺乏显式机制保护进化过程中发现的重要构建特征，导致有效特征被遗传算子破坏而丢失有价值遗传物质。

Method: 引入自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性度量在进化过程中选择性保留构建特征：对重要特征提供更强保护，同时允许较不重要特征被修改并从重要特征中融入有用的构建块。采用多种特征重要性计算方法，并在不同基学习器上验证鲁棒性。

Result: 在98个回归基准数据集上，该方法相比基线方法持续提升解质量；在两个信用分类数据集上证明了该方法能有效扩展至符号回归之外的搜索优化任务。

Conclusion: 基于特征重要性的自适应保护机制能有效解决进化特征构造中重要特征丢失问题，提升符号回归性能，并具有良好的泛化能力，可推广至其他进化优化应用场景。

Abstract: Evolutionary feature construction has shown strong promise in symbolic regression by automatically discovering informative transformations of input features that enhance a simple base learner. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to preserve important constructed features discovered during evolution, and valuable genetic material can be lost when genetic operators disrupt effective features. This paper introduces an adaptive protection mechanism that leverages feature importance metrics to selectively preserve constructed features during evolution. The mechanism provides stronger protection for more important constructed features while still allowing less important features to be modified and to incorporate useful building blocks from more important features. We evaluate the approach using multiple feature importance calculation methods and demonstrate its robustness across different base learners. Experimental results on 98 regression benchmark datasets show that the proposed mechanism consistently improves solution quality over baseline approaches, and experiments on two credit classification datasets demonstrate that the method also extends effectively to improve search effectiveness beyond symbolic regression.

</details>


### [51] [Training Fair Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14211)
*Patrik Kenfack,Jesse C. Cresswell,Anthony L. Caterini,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FairTFM，一种针对表格基础模型的公平性训练策略，通过合成公平性任务和梯度反转层，在保持竞争力的准确率的同时显著提升了模型公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型(TFMs)在高风险决策中的使用日益增加，但其公平性特性却很大程度上未被探索。现有公平性技术与上下文学习范式不兼容，且训练数据中敏感属性的访问受限，这构成了实现公平TFM的主要挑战。

Method: 提出FairTFM，一种可扩展的训练策略，包含两个核心组件：(1)基于合成公平性任务的训练方法，解决敏感属性访问受限的问题；(2)公平性感知架构，采用梯度反转层(GRL)，鼓励模型学习对敏感属性不变的表示。该方法将公平性约束直接集成到TFM训练中，实现在单次前向传播中进行公平预测。

Result: 在132个公平性任务上的实验表明，FairTFM在保持竞争性准确率的同时，公平性指标获得了持续且显著的改进。

Conclusion: FairTFM成功地将公平性约束融入表格基础模型的训练过程，有效解决了敏感属性访问受限和现有公平性技术与上下文学习不兼容的两大挑战，为高风险决策场景中的公平性预测提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as leading methods for tabular predictive tasks, leveraging in-context learning to predict on new data without task-specific training. Despite the increased use of TFMs in high-stakes decision-making, their fairness properties remain largely unexplored. In this work, we incorporate fairness constraints directly into TFM training, enabling fair predictions in a single forward pass. Our approach addresses two key challenges: limited access to sensitive attributes in training data, and the incompatibility of existing fairness techniques with the in-context learning paradigm. We propose FairTFM, a scalable training strategy based on synthetic fairness tasks and a fairness-aware architecture using a gradient reversal layer, which encourages the model to learn representations invariant to sensitive attributes. Experiments on 132 fairness tasks show consistent improvements in fairness while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [52] [Connected Subspace Clustering: Hardness, a Scalable Heuristic, and an Application to Sea Level Geodesy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14215)
*Johanna Hillebrand,Jan Höckendorff,Jürgen Kusche,Kelin Luo,Heiko Röglin,Melanie Schmidt,Christian Sohler,Bernd Uebbing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了连接子空间聚类问题及其高效启发式算法，用于求解带有连通性约束的聚类问题，在大地测量学等实际应用中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大地测量学应用中，需要对海平面时间序列进行主成分分析，识别连续的海面区域。传统无约束聚类方法会产生大量不连续的碎片，无法满足物理连贯性的需求，因此需要引入连通性约束来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出连接子空间聚类问题：给定高维点和连通图，将其划分为k个连通簇，最小化其到最佳拟合m'维仿射子空间的总平方距离。理论证明了问题的NP难近似性。提出Lloyd风格启发式算法，交替进行子空间拟合和迭代合并过程以强制连通性。

Result: 方法在构造上保证返回恰好k个连通区域，而无约束方法在高成本下留下多达1,966个不连续碎片。在160个全局海平面时间序列配置的研究中，合并修复策略在73.75%的情况下是四种策略中最强的，且在所有测试的聚类数上持续优于竞争方法。所得区域能分离出与厄尔尼诺-南方涛动和印度洋偶极子等气候指数一致的信号。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功解决了空间嵌入多元时间序列的连通性约束聚类问题，不仅适用于大地测量学，还可推广到气候场、遥感、神经影像和传感器网络等其他应用领域，为科学和工程领域的约束优化问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Constrained optimization extends classical optimization by integrating side information, making it widely applicable across scientific and engineering domains. Consider a setting where we measure variables at different physical locations. When grouping these measurements, we often want clusters that are both internally similar and physically coherent. Thus, we have a constrained clustering problem where the constraint models coherence. Motivated by an application in geodesy, where contiguous regions of the sea surface must be identified for principal component analysis, we introduce the Connected Subspace Clustering problem: given high-dimensional points and a connectivity graph, partition them into $k$ connected clusters, minimizing their total squared distance to the clusters' best-fit $m'$-dimensional affine subspaces. We prove that, even for $m' = 0$ and a grid graph with holes, the problem is NP-hard to approximate within $Ω(n^{1/2-\varepsilon})$ for every $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ is the number of measurements. We then introduce an efficient Lloyd-style heuristic that alternates subspace fitting with an iterative merging procedure to enforce connectivity. Our method returns exactly $k$ connected regions by construction, whereas unconstrained methods leave up to $1{,}966$ disconnected fragments at higher cost. In a study of 160 configurations on global sea level time series, our merging-based repair is the strongest of four strategies in $73.75\%$ of cases, and consistently outperforms competitors such as (connected) Ward's method across all tested cluster counts. The resulting regions isolate signals aligning with climate indices such as the El Nino-Southern Oscillation and Indian Ocean Dipole. Although developed for geodesy, the approach applies to other spatially embedded multivariate time series, such as climate fields, remote sensing, neuroimaging, and sensor networks.

</details>


### [53] [AutoSchema: Live Schema Grounding for Agentic Text-to-Sparql over Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14228)
*Yiming Zhang,Koji Tsuda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了无需训练的实时模式定位框架AUTOSCHEMA，使智能体能直接从知识图谱端点获取所需模式信息，在生物医学知识图谱问答任务上优于依赖人工维护文件的TogoMCP系统，并展现出对未见图谱的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命科学知识图谱虽通过SPARQL提供结构化数据，但各资源使用不同的模式、标识符和链接。现有方案（如TogoMCP）依赖人工维护的元数据互操作交换文件，创建和维护这些文件仍需语言模型辅助起草、验证和人工审查，成本高昂且更新滞后。

Method: 提出实时模式定位概念，智能体直接从当前端点获取问题所需的模式证据。开发了无需训练的通用框架AUTOSCHEMA，其通过检查实时模式、将问题中的实体名称映射到图标识符、探索关系路径、在迭代查询构建过程中发现资源间的可能连接来完成推理。

Result: 在生物医学KGQA任务中，AUTOSCHEMA的平均事实准确率超越TogoMCP；在纵向BioASQ评估中获得持续提升；减少了迭代预算耗尽情况，核心评估中平均工具调用次数更少。迁移研究表明该框架能支持不规则和先前未见的RDF图谱，无需预先创建人工管理的模式文件。

Conclusion: 实时模式定位方法有效降低了对人工维护元数据文件的依赖，提升了知识图谱问答的自动化水平和泛化能力，为处理未见知识图谱提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Life science knowledge graphs make large collections of structured data available through SPARQL, but each resource uses its own schema, identifiers, and links. TogoMCP helps language model agents query these resources by providing curated Metadata Interoperability Exchange files. Creating and maintaining these files still requires language model assisted drafting, validation, and manual review. We study \emph{live schema grounding}, where an agent obtains the schema evidence needed for a question directly from the current endpoints. We present \textsc{autoschema}, a general framework for live schema grounding that requires no training. It inspects live schemas, maps entity names in a question to graph identifiers, explores relation paths, and finds possible connections between resources during iterative query construction. We use TogoMCP as our main comparison framework. We evaluate \textsc{autoschema} on Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA, Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA, Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA over BioASQ Task B, and Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer to a previously undocumented RDF graph. \textsc{autoschema} improves mean factoid accuracy over TogoMCP in the biomedical KGQA tasks and gives consistent gains in the longitudinal BioASQ evaluation. It also reduces iteration budget exhaustion and uses fewer tool calls on average in the core evaluation. The transfer study gives preliminary evidence that live schema grounding can support irregular and previously unseen graphs without first creating a curated schema file.

</details>


### [54] [Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14264)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Vamshi Bonagiri,Raul Astudillo,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MOBO-Merge框架，通过多目标贝叶斯优化解决模型合并中的参数选择问题，在有限的评估预算下有效近似帕累托前沿，在12组对比实验中有11组获得了比随机搜索更高的平均超体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并是一种计算高效的模型融合方法，但合并参数的选择面临三大挑战：下游评估成本高昂、梯度信息不可用、源模型能力可能冲突。现有方法缺乏有效的参数搜索机制，亟需一种黑盒优化方法来自动选择最优合并参数。

Method: 将合并参数选择建模为黑盒多目标优化问题，提出MOBO-Merge框架。该框架与合并算子无关，采用多目标贝叶斯优化在有限评估预算下近似帕累托前沿。实验涵盖了Linear、SLERP、TIES和块级合并算子，在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B模型上进行两模型（指令-数学）和三模型（指令-数学-代码）合并设置评估。

Result: 在留出基准测试分区上，MOBO-Merge在12组对比实验中的11组获得了比随机搜索更高的平均超体积。增益在单维Linear插值中较小，但在多组TIES、块级合并和三目标搜索中显著更大。不同合并算子各有优势：TIES在四种族-设置组合中的三种表现最佳，而Block-Linear 4x在Llama三模型合并中最强。

Conclusion: 多目标贝叶斯优化作为表达性合并参数化的搜索层具有重要价值。不存在普遍最优的合并算子，不同场景需要选择不同的合并策略。MOBO-Merge为模型合并提供了一种高效、通用的参数优化方案。

Abstract: Model merging combines trained models directly in weight space, offering a compute-efficient alternative to additional fine-tuning. Selecting merge parameters is nevertheless difficult because downstream evaluations are expensive, gradients are unavailable, and source capabilities can conflict. We formulate merge-parameter selection as a black-box multi-objective optimization problem and introduce MOBO-Merge, a merge-operator agnostic framework that uses multi-objective Bayesian optimization to approximate the Pareto front under a limited evaluation budget. We evaluate Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B in two-model instruction-math and three-model instruction-math-code settings using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and block-wise merge operators. On held-out benchmark partitions, MOBO-Merge obtains higher mean hypervolume than random search in 11 of 12 reported comparisons. The gain is small for one-dimensional Linear interpolation but substantially larger for several TIES, block-wise, and three-objective searches. No merge operator is uniformly best: TIES leads in three of four family-setting combinations, whereas Block-Linear 4x is strongest for the Llama three-model merge. These results show that multi-objective Bayesian optimization is valuable as a search layer for expressive merge parameterizations.

</details>


### [55] [Convex losses and their applications to SVM, SVR, and Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288)
*Filippo Portera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多种用于SVM和神经网络的新型凸损失函数，但在实验中未发现其能显著提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过在损失函数中引入模式相关性，探索是否能提升二分类任务中SVM和神经网络的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了多种新型凸损失函数，证明了其是标准损失函数的推广；对于SVM原始问题，采用粒子群优化算法求解；通过嵌套交叉验证评估性能。

Result: 新型损失函数在理论上可能提升部分数据集的泛化性能，但实验结果表明，使用新损失函数与不使用时的泛化性能指标基本相同。

Conclusion: 所提出的凸损失函数是标准损失的有效推广，但在实际应用中未能显著改善模型的泛化能力，相关工作为后续研究提供了参考。

Abstract: We propose multiple new convex losses for SVM and Neural Networks, applied to binary classification tasks. While there are practical limitations in exploiting them with the dual SVM models, we are able to use them with SVM primal formulation and Neural Networks. In detail, the primal SVM problem with the modified losses has been solved with the Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm. We prove that the proposed losses are a generalization of the standard loss, and we experiment them with several small data-sets. This preliminary study shows that using pattern correlations
  inside the loss function could in theory enhance the generalization performances on some data-sets. To evaluate the performance of each loss, we adopt a Nested Cross-Validation procedure. Results show that generalization measures are the same with or without the new losses.

</details>


### [56] [Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303)
*Francesco Quinzan,Noor Munir,Yishun Lu,Stephen Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CodeSIFT，一种不依赖特定威胁模型的检测方法，利用影响函数识别诱导大语言模型生成不安全代码的恶意提示，在三个开源代码LLM上实现了高达0.98的AUROC分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对代码生成的防御方法通常依赖预定义的威胁模型或已知漏洞模式，这限制了它们应对新型攻击的有效性。需要一个不依赖先验知识的通用检测框架来识别恶意代码生成提示。

Method: 提出CodeSIFT方法：1) 利用影响函数测量生成代码的参数空间影响；2) 使用统计检验判断候选提示集是否偏离良性参考分布；3) 引入两个覆盖多种漏洞的基准数据集进行评估。

Result: 在参数规模3B-7B的三个开源代码LLM上评估，在中高注入率下达到高达0.98的AUROC分数，保持良好校准的假阳性率，显著优于静态分析基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于影响函数的检测方法是识别恶意代码生成提示的有前景方向，无需预先了解攻击类别即可有效检测，为代码生成安全提供了新的防御思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet they remain vulnerable to prompts that elicit insecure implementations. Existing defenses typically rely on predefined threat models or known vulnerability patterns, limiting their effectiveness against novel attacks. We propose CodeSIFT, a threat-model-agnostic detection method that leverages influence functions to identify batches of prompts that induce anomalous model behavior. Rather than detecting specific vulnerabilities, CodeSIFT measures the parameter-space influence of generated code and uses a statistical test to determine whether a candidate prompt set deviates from a benign reference distribution. To evaluate our approach, we introduce two benchmark datasets covering a variety of vulnerabilities. We evaluate CodeSIFT on three open-weight code LLMs ranging from 3B to 7B parameters, achieving AUROC scores of up to 0.98 at moderate-to-high injection rates, while maintaining well-calibrated false positive rates and substantially outperforming static analysis baselines. These results suggest that influence-function-based detection is a promising direction for identifying malicious code-generation prompts without requiring prior knowledge of the underlying attack class.

</details>


### [57] [Quantum Multi-Armed Bandits and Linear Bandits: Lower Bounds and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14319)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为量子多臂老虎机(QMAB)和量子线性老虎机(QLB)建立了首个极小化极大下界，并提出改进算法，解决了Wan et al. [2023]提出的关于T无关遗憾是否可达的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作给出了QMAB的O(K log T)遗憾界和QLB的O(d² polylog T)遗憾界，但遗留两个核心问题：(1) K log T的规模是否不可避免；(2) d²依赖关系是否可以改进。Wan et al. [2023]提出了T无关遗憾是否可实现的开放问题。

Method: 核心技术贡献包括：(1) 利用多项式方法和三角多项式的Remez型不等式，证明高置信度单臂量子测试下界，用于区分固定奖励均值与备选区间；(2) 通过老虎机到测试的归约将测试下界提升为QMAB下界；(3) 利用线性嵌入技术获得有限动作QLB下界；(4) 提出基于设计的消去算法，将低偏差低方差量子均值估计器与小支撑G-最优设计耦合，通过匹配设计权重的查询分配实现改进。

Result: 证明了QMAB的Ω(K log(T/K))极小化极大下界和有限动作QLB的Ω(d log(T/d))下界；对于多项式规模动作集的有限动作QLB，算法遗憾线性依赖于d，改进了之前的d²依赖，在多对数因子内匹配下界；使用量子蒙特卡洛估计时，基于设计的消去算法将维度依赖从d²降至d^{3/2}。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Wan et al. [2023]提出的关于T无关遗憾是否可实现的开放问题，证明了其不可行性；建立了量子老虎机问题的理论下界，并设计了最优算法匹配这些下界（在多对数因子内），为量子老虎机领域提供了完整的理论刻画。

Abstract: We study quantum multi-armed bandits (QMAB) and quantum linear bandits (QLB) in the model of Wan et al. [2023], where the learner queries each arm or action through a quantum reward oracle or its inverse. Prior work gives algorithms over horizon $T$ with regret $O(K\log T)$ for QMAB with $K$ arms and $O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$ for $d$-dimensional QLB. This leaves open whether the $K\log T$ scale is unavoidable and whether the $d^2$ dependence can be improved. We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $Ω(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $Ω(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. A bandit-to-testing reduction then lifts it to the QMAB lower bound, while a linear embedding gives the finite-action QLB lower bound. Complementing the lower bounds, we give a design-based elimination algorithm for finite-action QLB. When the action set has size $\operatorname{poly}(d)$, its regret is linear in $d$, improving the prior $d^2$ dependence and matching our lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The algorithm couples a low-bias low-variance quantum mean estimator with a small-support $G$-optimal design through a query allocation matched to the design weights. The design-based elimination reduces the dimension dependence from $d^2$ to $d^{3/2}$ when using Quantum Monte Carlo estimates. The low-variance estimator then makes reconstruction error aggregate through variance rather than worst-case absolute error, removing the remaining $\sqrt d$ factor.

</details>


### [58] [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349)
*Michael Fore,Akshay Jain,Justin Downes,Rohan Pradhan,Duncan Botti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的多模态轨迹预测方法，在零学习参数和无GPU条件下达到5700万参数Transformer的精度，在数据稀缺场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的轨迹预测方法依赖大量训练数据和GPU资源，在数据稀缺的新地理区域部署困难。需要一种能够在数据有限条件下实现高效轨迹预测的方法。

Method: 构建历史状态-下一位置对的转移表，使用包含空间邻近性、方位、速度和时间上下文的产品核函数检索邻居。提供两种推理模式：多样性惩罚采样生成覆盖不同合理路线的轨迹；束搜索找到最高似然路径。无需训练，零学习参数。

Result: 在TrAISformer基准（丹麦海事AIS数据）上，在完整数据条件下取得竞争性精度；在数据稀缺场景下显著优于Transformer，仅用10%训练数据仍保持稳定，而TrAISformer性能严重退化。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了从数量级更少的历史数据部署到新地理区域的能力，无需GPU训练，为数据稀缺场景下的轨迹预测提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: We present a training-free method for multi-modal trajectory prediction that achieves comparable accuracy to a 57M-parameter transformer while requiring no GPU and zero learned parameters. The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical data, and with no GPU training.

</details>


### [59] [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367)
*Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析14个事件日志，揭示了业务流程延迟检测的内在困难：延迟时间呈右偏分布，现有模型在长延迟样本上表现不佳且预测不确定性随延迟程度增加。研究表明不确定性感知建模可显著提升延迟案例识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管剩余时间预测技术已显著进步，但学术界对延迟检测本身的内在难度缺乏深入了解。现有研究多使用聚合指标评估性能，难以洞察模型在不同目标分布（尤其是运营关键的大延迟样本）上的表现差异。

Method: 基于14个事件日志系统分析延迟检测的难度，揭示剩余时间的右偏分布特征、模型在高峰值案例上的性能差异以及预测不确定性随延迟程度增加的异方差性。评估了不平衡问题缓解方法的有效性，并探索利用不确定性与延迟的相关性改进延迟案例识别。

Result: 发现延迟时间呈强右偏分布，长延迟样本占比极小；现有模型能较好捕捉分布模态但在大延迟案例上性能显著下降；预测不确定性与延迟幅度呈正相关（异方差性）；不平衡缓解方法效果有限，表明核心问题在于延迟案例固有的高不确定性而非数据不平衡。利用不确定性相关性可显著提升延迟案例识别准确率。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了延迟检测困难的根源，指出不确定性感知建模是未来预测性流程监控研究的重要方向，为提升延迟检测性能提供了新视角和方法路径。

Abstract: The early detection of delayed cases in business processes is a critical capability for organizations. Predictive process monitoring (PPM) supports this task by using historical event logs to predict the remaining time of ongoing cases, enabling timely interventions to avoid missed deadlines and service level violations. Although remaining time prediction has advanced considerably through sophisticated deep learning architectures, little is known about the intrinsic difficulty of delay detection itself. Since performance is typically assessed using aggregate metrics, prior work provides limited insight into how models perform across the target distribution, especially on the operationally most critical cases with large delays. In this paper, we address this gap by analyzing the difficulty of delay detection. Across 14 event logs, we show that remaining times are typically strongly right-skewed, with only a small fraction of cases exhibiting large delays. Existing models capture the mode of this distribution well but perform poorly on high-delay cases. We further uncover pronounced heteroscedasticity, showing that predictive uncertainty increases with delay magnitude. Based on these findings, we evaluate approaches to mitigate the imbalance problem, but find only limited benefits, suggesting that the key underlying problem may not be imbalance but higher uncertainty associated with delayed cases. We show that this correlation can be exploited to substantially improve the identification of delayed cases. Overall, our work provides new insights into the sources of difficulty in delay detection and identifies uncertainty-aware modeling as a promising direction for future PPM research.

</details>


### [60] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了imposter这一判别性前置任务，通过特征替换利用物理一致性进行自监督学习。实验表明不同下游任务需要不同的前置任务，imposter与现有SSL目标结合能提供互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据描述的实体特征由物理定律共同支配，但现有的自监督学习（SSL）目标在很大程度上忽略了这种物理一致性。现有SSL方法无法有效捕捉跨特征的物理依赖关系。

Method: 提出imposter方法：一种判别性前置任务，将实体特征子集替换为另一个实体捐赠的真实观测值，并训练编码器识别被交换的特征。由于每个捐赠值单独看都是合理的，只有通过学习跨特征物理依赖关系才能解决该任务。使用共享架构和预训练预算，对21个环境变量的ERA5-Land再分析数据进行系统比较。

Result: 在七个下游任务（气候分类、碳通量估算、径流预测）上评估学习表示。发现最有效的前置任务取决于下游任务家族而非单一目标的优越性；imposter与现有SSL目标结合时提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学基础模型自监督学习有价值的新来源。不同下游任务族需要不同的前置任务，imposter为现有SSL方法提供了补充性的物理信息。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [61] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明随机权重平均(SWA)在增强数据上训练时，能有效提升模型等变性，且在无限宽度极限下超越SWA本身的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度集成在增强数据上训练能实现完美对称性，但需要多次重复训练，计算成本高昂。需要一种无需重复训练的高效等变集成方法。

Method: 使用随机权重平均(SWA)作为替代集成技术，通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练末期的随机轨迹，在无限宽度极限下分析SWA在增强数据上的等变性。

Result: 在无限宽度极限下，SWA在增强数据上提供的等变性提升超越了SWA本身带来的性能增益；在计算机视觉和图分类任务上的大量数值实验验证了该结论。

Conclusion: SWA是一种无需重复训练的高效方法，能在增强数据训练中有效提升模型的等变性，为对称性感知的深度学习提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [62] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeaMoE架构，通过专家分组部门化和参数共享机制，结合定制两阶段路由策略，有效解决小批量解码场景下MoE推理的内存瓶颈问题，显著提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型在实时交互应用（如编程助手、音视频交互系统）的小批量解码场景下，推理过程成为内存受限型，严重受限于专家权重加载瓶颈。现有解决方案（如训练后权重压缩或训练时的细粒度专家设计）要么损害模型精度，要么引入额外计算和通信开销。

Method: 提出DeaMoE架构：1) 将专家按专业领域分组为多个"部门"，同一部门内的专家共享大部分参数，仅保留少量私有参数体现个体独特性；2) 设计定制的两阶段路由策略避免冗余加载，提升LLM解码效率。

Result: 相比原生MoE，DeaMoE将每步加载权重量减少高达50.9%，在A40上对预训练7B模型实现最高1.33倍端到端TPOT加速，微基准测试中DeepSeek-V3在A40和H100上分别实现最高2.00倍和1.97倍峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过专家部门化和参数共享机制，在不损害模型精度的前提下有效缓解小批量解码场景下的内存瓶颈问题，为实时交互应用提供了一种高效可行的MoE推理加速方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [63] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CytoBERT，一个针对单细胞流式细胞术数据的开源基础模型，通过自监督预训练学习可迁移的标记间关系，实现了跨异质性数据集的迁移学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式细胞术数据由于实验方案和测量特征选择的差异，存在高度异构和缺乏标准化的问题，导致机器学习方法难以在不同研究间应用和迁移，而该领域缺乏相应的基础模型方法。

Method: 构建CytoBERT基础模型，在包含15个人类数据集、超过5000万个细胞的大规模流式细胞术语料库上进行自监督预训练，通过标记标准化技术学习细胞内可迁移的标记间关系。

Result: 在样本级分类任务的微调实验中，证明了跨异质性流式细胞术数据集的迁移学习是可行的，为可扩展、可泛化的流式细胞术分析提供了起点。

Conclusion: CytoBERT成功解决了流式细胞术数据异构性带来的迁移学习挑战，为该领域提供了一个可扩展、可泛化的分析基础，代码已开源以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [64] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了Power Sampling在推理任务中的一个反直觉现象：虽然它能增加正确轨迹的概率质量，却可能导致下游推理性能下降。作者通过识别“剂量失配”和“覆盖失配”两个核心问题，提出了一种变形可控、支持保持的Power目标方法，成功逆转了性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling被提出作为一种无需验证器的推理增强方法，有望成为多种下游采样方法的前端。然而，作者发现了一个关键矛盾：Power Sampling虽然能将更多概率质量导向正确轨迹，却会损害其原本意图增强的下游推理性能，如在自一致性任务中观察到高达18.5个百分点的准确率下降。这一反直觉现象促使作者深入分析其根本原因。

Method: 作者首先诊断出导致性能下降的两个核心失配机制：1）剂量失配，由固定指数导致不同问题间分布变化程度差异巨大；2）覆盖失配，全局锐化将概率质量集中于少数主导路径，导致下游聚合、搜索和选择所需的广泛推理路径支持丧失。基于此诊断，作者提出了改进方案：用变形可控、支持保持的Power目标替代均匀轨迹指数化，在问题间校准锐化程度的同时限制对中等概率路径的抑制，并结合加权重自一致性进行实现。

Result: 在相同预算设置下，改进后的采样器成功逆转了全局Power导致的性能损失，并在多个推理基准测试上超越了标准多样本推理方法。实验验证了该方法在保持性能提升的同时避免了原有Power Sampling的缺陷。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了推理时分布锐化方法中的一个根本性悖论：看似提升正确性的操作可能暗中损害下游任务所需的多样性支持。作者提出的变形可控、支持保持的解决方案有效解决了这一问题，为设计既能增强推理能力又能保持路径多样性的采样方法提供了重要指导原则。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [65] [Designing Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models: A Unified Path-Space View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14430)
*Yixian Xu,Yuanrui Zhang,Shengjie Luo,Liwei Wang,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散模型的两类强化学习方法（反向轨迹法和前向匹配法）实际上源于同一路径空间原理，其性能差异主要源于方差缩减效应而非原理不同，并提出了改进的统一算法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型的强化学习后训练算法呈现碎片化状态：反向轨迹方法依赖离散化似然比，而前向匹配方法在带奖励标签的去噪样本上训练。这些看似不同的方法缺乏统一的理论框架，限制了算法设计和性能提升。

Method: 从正则化扩散-RL目标出发，利用采样SDE之间的重要性采样得到轨迹空间上的显式策略梯度估计器。该估计器包含Flow-GRPO型更新的随机Itô积分，进一步推导出等价的方差缩减值梯度形式，从而恢复AWM和DiffusionNFT的前向匹配结构。基于此构建统一设计空间，提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器和有界权重族。

Result: 实验验证了两类方法家族之间的经验差距确实是方差缩减效应。在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image模型上的实验表明，提出的统一算法框架相比先前的扩散-RL基线方法取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文建立了扩散模型强化学习的统一理论框架，证明了现有方法源于同一原理，性能差异主要来自方差缩减。该统一视角为未来算法设计提供了清晰的设计空间，提出的改进方法验证了理论发现的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training provides a direct way to align diffusion models with human preferences and task-specific rewards. However, current RL algorithms for diffusion models remain fragmented: reverse-trajectory methods rely on discretized likelihood ratios, whereas forward-matching methods train on reward-labeled noising versions of the rollout samples. This paper shows that these seemingly different losses arise from a single path-space principle. Starting from the regularized diffusion-RL objective, we use importance sampling between sampling SDEs to obtain an explicit policy-gradient estimator on trajectory space. The estimator contains the stochastic Itô integral underlying Flow-GRPO-type updates; we derive an equivalent variance-reduced value-gradient form that recovers the forward-matching structure of AWM and DiffusionNFT. This identifies the empirical gap between these method families as a variance-reduction effect rather than a difference in RL principle. The derivation yields a unified design space organized by value-gradient estimation, weight functions, and sampling choices. Within this space, we propose a multi-sample KDE value-gradient estimator that reuses rollout groups, together with scale-bounded weight families that retain stable existing recipes while excluding singular ones. Experiments on SD3.5-M and Qwen-Image models validate the variance-reduction explanation and show that the resulting recipe improves over prior diffusion-RL baselines.

</details>


### [66] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于双层优化框架的神经架构搜索方法，通过连续松弛技术替代离散架构决策，实现了高效且可扩展的架构搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索(NAS)通常被建模为双层优化问题,但面临计算成本高昂的挑战,主要源于离散架构决策、指数级增长的搜索空间以及训练候选架构的高昂代价。现有的离散搜索方法效率低下,亟需开发可扩展的连续优化框架来降低搜索成本并提高搜索效率。

Method: 提出了一种通用的双层优化框架,适用于MLPs、CNNs、RNNs和Transformers等多种架构。核心创新是三种可扩展的连续松弛方法:将离散的神经元级和激活级决策替换为连续松弛,从而在组合架构空间上实现可微分优化。基于此提出了三种NAS方法:基于神经元门控的NAS(NG)、基于混合激活的NAS(MA)以及结合两者的NAS-NGMA。

Result: 在MNIST数据集上,NAS-NGMA实现了98.68%测试准确率和7.69M参数量,NAS-NG在CNN上达到99.63%准确率仅需0.26M参数。在CIFAR-10上,提出的方法持续优于标准DARTS方法。实验证明NAS-NG能够优化过度参数化和文献中最优的架构,在减少参数的同时提高准确率,验证了松弛双层优化作为离散NAS可扩展替代方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 松弛双层优化为高效神经元级和激活级架构优化提供了通用框架,成功克服了传统离散NAS的计算瓶颈,实现了紧凑且高性能架构的自动搜索,为NAS领域提供了新的可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [67] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一种基于线性规划的神经网络架构搜索框架，通过计算验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian矩阵来优化架构更新方向，在CIFAR和ImageNet数据集上实现了比现有方法更快的收敛速度和更高的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可微分神经网络架构搜索方法虽然比传统NAS方法更高效准确，但在架构搜索空间的优化过程中仍有改进空间。可微分NAS将架构搜索空间松弛到连续域后，可以应用连续优化原理来改进架构搜索效率和效果。

Method: 提出基于线性规划的神经网络架构搜索框架（LP-NAS），该方法利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian矩阵构建线性规划问题，计算能够提高泛化能力同时保持模型参数最优性的架构更新方向。引入两种计算高效的变体：S-LP-NAS和R-LP-NAS，并应用于DARTS搜索空间，形成S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上的实验表明：LP-DARTS在架构搜索和评估阶段均优于标准DARTS算法；在搜索早期迭代中实现更快收敛和显著更高的验证性能；与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS和STO-DARTS等DARTS变体相比表现出优越性；在ImageNet数据集上验证了所发现架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: LP-NAS成功地将线性规划理论应用于可微分神经网络架构搜索，通过优化架构更新方向实现了更高效准确的架构优化。该方法具有良好的通用性，可应用于广泛的连续搜索空间，为神经网络架构自动化设计提供了新的理论视角和实践方法。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [68] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了sMuon方法，通过线性化和最小二乘近似解决了Muon优化器无法与低秩参数化（如LoRA）结合的问题，在参数高效微调中实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在预训练中表现优异，但在参数高效微调（PEFT）中应用较少。主要原因是最常用的PEFT方法LoRA无法与Muon自然结合——低秩参数化产生的权重更新在数学上无法进行正交化处理。

Method: 通过线性化近似松弛后的Muon目标函数，利用最小二乘法在低秩设置下求解近似解。实现了仅需矩阵乘法运算的高效算法，避免了复杂的线性代数分解操作，提出了sMuon（small Muon）方法。

Result: 在监督微调（SFT）和ReLoRA预训练实验中，sMuon表现良好。虽然结果依赖于具体模型和评估指标，但整体上Muon用于低秩微调能够带来适度的性能提升。

Conclusion: 成功解决了Muon优化器与低秩参数化方法（如LoRA）的不兼容问题，为参数高效微调提供了一种新的优化器选择，能够带来稳定的性能改进。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [69] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段的跨表数据生成（CTDG）框架，用于从多个异质表格学习统一的生成模型，以生成高质量合成异质表格数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成表格数据生成方法主要局限于单一输入表场景，难以有效处理具有多样化特征集的多个异质表格。跨表数据生成（CTDG）旨在解决从多个异质表格学习生成模型并产生新的合成表格数据集的挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段将每个异质原始表转换为标准化的统计表，统计表具有相同的列集，捕获原始列的边际分布及其之间的成对相关性；第二阶段训练扩散Transformer模型，捕获这些同质统计表间的结构模式并生成合成统计表；最后通过多元高斯采样结合逆概率积分变换从生成的统计表重建合成原始表。

Result: 实验结果表明，所学习的统计表示具有高保真度，生成的合成数据在保真度与多样性之间取得了良好平衡，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架能够从多个异质表格学习统一生成模型，并支持生成不限数量的逼真合成异质表格，为跨表数据生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [70] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RecipeNet，一种分层Transformer架构，用于配方数据的表示学习，在多个数据集和任务上优于现有表格模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格学习方法通常将配方数据的分层结构（有序步骤序列）扁平化为固定模式表示，限制了其捕获字段间层次交互和过程依赖关系的能力。

Method: 提出RecipeNet，一种分层Transformer架构，通过堆叠的Transformer编码器编码每步内的字段级交互和跨步的过程依赖关系。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet始终优于现有的表格模型。

Conclusion: 分层和序列建模对配方表示学习具有重要价值。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>

### [71] [A Bounded Reclaim Actuator for PSI-Guided Compressed Memory: A Controlled Ablation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13689)
*Abhiyan Dhakal,Sanjog Sigdel*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本研究通过控制实验比较了Linux内存压力管理中zram、PSI和cgroup v2三种机制的三种配置策略，发现静态zram配合一次性cgroup回收请求能降低计算负载6%的P99响应时间，而延迟激活zram效果较差，表明优化效果取决于具体工作负载特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux提供了PSI（压力停滞信息）、zram（压缩内存交换空间）和cgroup v2（控制组内存回收）等多种内存压力管理机制，这些机制常被同时讨论但实际作用于压力路径的不同阶段。当前缺乏对这些机制配置策略的系统性比较研究，难以确定最佳实践。

Method: 采用控制系统研究方法，在9台1-vCPU Linux虚拟机上对比三种配置：(1)启动时启用zram；(2)PSI检测到内存压力后才启用zram；(3)启动时启用zram并执行一次96 MiB的cgroup回收请求。先通过16个试点案例确定回收请求大小，再运行180个确认案例（每组60个），使用计算负载和SQLite负载进行测试。

Result: 与静态zram配置相比，有界回收配置使计算负载的P99响应时间降低6%，而SQLite负载结果统计上无显著差异。延迟激活配置的P99延迟高于其他两种方案。结果表明优化收益取决于前台工作负载及其内存访问路径，而非普遍适用于所有工作负载。

Conclusion: Linux内存压力管理机制的效果具有工作负载依赖性，不存在通用的最佳配置策略。静态zram配合适度的cgroup回收请求对计算密集型负载有效，而延迟激活策略表现不佳。实践中的配置选择应基于具体工作负载特征进行针对性优化。

Abstract: When the aggregate working set of active processes exceeds physical RAM capacity, the machine experiences memory pressure. Applications may therefore slow down before the kernel kills a process. Linux provides several ways to observe and respond: Pressure Stall Information (PSI) can detect memory-related task stalls, zram can provide compressed in-memory swap space, and cgroup v2 can request memory reclamation within a selected control group. These facilities are often discussed together even though they act at different points in the pressure path. This paper examines that distinction with a controlled systems study. We compare three setups: zram enabled from startup; zram enabled only after PSI indicates memory pressure; and zram enabled from startup with a one-time 96 MiB cgroup reclaim request. We first selected the request size in a 16-case pilot, then ran 180 confirmatory cases, 60 cases for each setup, on nine 1-vCPU Linux virtual machines with compute and SQLite workloads. Compared with static zram, the bounded reclaim configuration reduced compute p99 response time by 6\%, while the SQLite result was statistically indistinguishable. Delayed activation had higher median p99 latency than both alternatives. These results suggest that the benefit depends on the foreground workload and its memory-access path, rather than a general improvement across workloads.

</details>


### [72] [CoRun: Padding is Simple and Efficient for Deterministic LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14376)
*Shiju Zhao,Jiacheng Yang,Qihang Chen,Junhao Hu,Jiaqi Zheng,Guihai Chen,Xusheng Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRun系统，通过调度策略而非批不变内核来实现LLM推理的确定性，在保证输出一致性的同时显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理在固定采样参数和随机种子下仍存在输出不一致问题，主要源于批依赖的GPU执行（动态输入形状改变内核分块和浮点归约顺序）。现有批不变内核方案虽然解决了确定性问题，但限制了优化分块和分割归约，导致延迟增加2倍以上、吞吐量下降高达74%。

Method: 观察到大多数内核虽非批不变，但具有位置不变性。基于此提出CoRun调度系统：(1)采用隔离的预填充阶段和固定形状批量解码阶段处理LLM推理的两个阶段；(2)利用CUDA graphs实现高效执行和简化实现；(3)无需批不变内核即可实现确定性推理。

Result: 在包括Qwen和DeepSeek等多样化架构的LLM上的实验表明：CoRun在确保确定性的同时，相比批不变方法吞吐量提升15-324%，首次令牌时间平均减少51.8%，每输出令牌时间平均减少48.6%。

Conclusion: CoRun成功证明了通过调度策略可以在不牺牲性能的前提下实现LLM推理的确定性，为解决LLM推理不一致问题提供了高效实用的方案，克服了现有批不变内核方法的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Despite fixed sampling parameters and random seeds, Large Language Model (LLM) inference exhibits output inconsistency, which undermines downstream tasks such as model evaluation and reinforcement learning. A major source of this nondeterminism is batch-dependent GPU execution: dynamic input shapes change kernel tiling and floating-point reduction orders. Existing systems address this problem with batch-invariant kernels, but these kernels restrict optimized tiling and split reductions, increasing more than 2$\times$ latency and reducing serving throughput by up to 74 %. This paper observes that although most kernels are not batch-invariant, they are position-invariant. Leveraging this property, we present CoRun, a scheduling-based system that achieves deterministic inference without requiring batch invariance. CoRun employs isolated prefill and fixed-shape batched decode to handle the two stages of LLM inference, respectively, leveraging CUDA graphs for efficient execution and simplified implementation. Experiments on LLMs with diverse architectures, including Qwen and DeepSeek, show that CoRun ensures determinism while improving throughput by 15-324 % over batch-invariant approaches, reducing time-to-first-token by 51.8 % and time-per-output-token by 48.6 % on average.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Adaptive Snapshots Require Visible Reads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13705)
*Niv Sulimany,Tomer Cory,Erez Petrank*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在单写入者单扫描器设置下，满足现有自适应快照实现假设的带不可见读的自适应快照无法满足线性化一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应快照实现均未采用不可见读技术，而不可见读可显著提升性能，因此研究带不可见读的自适应快照是否可行成为关键问题。

Method: 理论证明方法：在单写入者、单扫描器设置下，基于现有自适应快照实现所满足的合理假设，分析带不可见读的自适应快照的线性化性质。

Result: 证明在满足现有实现假设条件下，带不可见读的自适应快照无法实现线性化一致性，即使限制在单写入者、单扫描器设置下亦然。

Conclusion: 不可见读技术与自适应快照的线性化要求存在根本冲突，为未来自适应快照算法设计提供了重要理论界限。

Abstract: Snapshots are widely used to record the state of a running execution. Snapshots have been extensively studied in the literature, with the goal of improving performance and extending functionality. In this work, we consider $adaptive$ snapshots over a set of $m$ components. Adaptive snapshots provide a Click() operation that logically creates a new snapshot and an Observe$(i)$ operation that returns the state of component $i$ at the most recent Click(). Several constructions of adaptive snapshots have recently been proposed; interestingly, none of them employs invisible reads, even though invisible reads can improve performance, sometimes significantly. In this paper, we ask whether it is possible to build an adaptive snapshot with invisible reads. We show that, even when restricting the snapshot algorithm to the single-writer, single-scanner setting, under reasonable assumptions satisfied by all existing adaptive snapshot implementations, adaptive snapshots with invisible reads are not linearizable.

</details>


### [74] [Balancing Workload Performance and Slurm Stress: Four Nextflow Deployment Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13824)
*Nil Tianchen Mu,William Dizon,Glen Otero,Torey Battelle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的测量协议和基准测试工具，用于评估在共享Slurm集群上运行大规模Nextflow工作流的不同部署策略，发现任务墙钟时间与调度器RPC负载之间存在权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享Slurm集群上，大规模Nextflow工作流可能提交数万个短任务，不同的部署策略（独立作业、任务数组或嵌套调度器）会影响工作流周转时间和RPC负载。现有研究要么比较整体工作流系统，要么仅关注单一架构的任务排队指标，缺乏跨架构的统一评估方法。

Method: 提出了一个可复现的测量协议和基准测试工具，包含两个关键要素：(1) 清零启动时钟，在任何后端服务或分配请求之前开始计时，将不同架构置于统一的时间轴上；(2) 使用Slurm sdiag计数器测量每个用户的RPC负载，将控制器处理时间作为独立于集群整体状态的敏感性上下文。在ASU Phoenix生产集群和单用户Dev环境中评估了四种多节点策略：Slurm原生、Slurm任务数组、HyperQueue和Flux。

Result: 在包含4,823个LASTZ任务的固定工作负载上，结果显示墙钟时间与RPC负载之间存在权衡关系。在Phoenix集群上，任务数组和HyperQueue在0.32小时内完成任务，Flux需要0.65小时但将RPC负载降至每千个任务1,396次。在Dev环境中，任务数组用时0.80小时，Flux用时0.84小时但将RPC负载降至每千个任务1,769次。三种策略均未被支配，形成了性能前沿。

Conclusion: 该方法使HPC站点能够使用同时捕获用户体验和调度器影响的指标来比较部署策略，并能在站点特定的RPC预算约束内选择最快的策略，平衡工作流效率和集群调度器负载。

Abstract: Wide Nextflow fan-outs on shared Slurm clusters can submit tens of thousands of short tasks. Deployment choices - individual jobs, arrays, or nested schedulers within allocations - affect both workflow turnaround and RPC volume, a shared cost that can degrade scheduler responsiveness. Existing studies compare whole workflow systems, while per-task queueing metrics do not span architectures that dispatch inside an existing allocation. We present a reproducible measurement protocol and bench harness with two key elements. First, a clean-start clock begins before any backend service or allocation request, placing different architectures on a common time axis. Second, per-user Slurm sdiag counters measure attributable RPC demand, with controller processing time reported as a sensitivity context separate from cluster-wide state. We evaluate four multi-node strategies: Slurm native, Slurm job arrays, HyperQueue, and Flux, on the shared ASU Phoenix production cluster; the single-user Dev campaign includes native, arrays, and Flux. The fixed workload contains 4,823 LASTZ tasks, with one clean-start trial per configuration in this preliminary campaign. Results show a walltime-RPC trade-off. On Phoenix, arrays and HyperQueue reached the milestone in 0.32 h, while Flux took 0.65 h but reduced attributable RPCs to 1,396 per 1,000 terminal tasks; all three were non-dominated. On Dev, arrays and Flux formed the observed frontier: 0.80 h for arrays versus 0.84 h for Flux, with Flux reducing RPCs to 1,769 per 1,000 terminal tasks. The method enables HPC sites to compare deployment strategies using metrics that capture both user experience and scheduler impact, and to choose the fastest strategy within a site-specific RPC budget.

</details>


### [75] [DDB: Source-Level Interactive Debugging for Distributed Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06107)
*Yibo Yan,Junzhou He,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DDB，一个针对分布式系统的源码级交互式调试器，通过分布式回溯、意图保持控制平面和时间虚拟化技术，解决了传统调试器在分布式环境中面临的跨进程调用栈断裂、基础设施动态性和超时级联等关键挑战，实现了对分布式应用的高效交互式调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互式调试器仅适用于单进程执行，在分布式系统中面临三大核心挑战：调用栈在进程边界处中断、调试状态无法在基础设施动态变化中存活、以及调试器引发的执行暂停会触发灾难性的超时级联，破坏调试流程。这迫使开发者放弃实时的假设检验，转而采用繁琐的日志-重部署迭代循环，降低了开发效率。

Method: 提出了DDB系统，包含三项关键技术：(1) 分布式回溯(DBT)：在每个RPC中嵌入紧凑的因果元数据，重建跨RPC边界的统一调用栈；(2) 意图保持控制平面：自动协调并在动态进程集中传播断点，管理分布式会话生命周期；(3) 暂停擦除时间(PET)：虚拟化每个进程的时钟，将逻辑时间与物理暂停解耦，防止超时级联。系统仅需20-60行代码即可与RPC框架集成。

Result: 在gRPC、ServiceWeaver、Nu和Quicksand框架上测试（最多122个进程），DDB实现了30毫秒的中位跨RPC回溯延迟，重复执行暂停下的亚5毫秒时间跳跃，以及1-5%的吞吐量开销，与单进程调试器相当。在对照用户研究中，DDB达到100%的故障定位成功率（基线工具为38.5%），中位定位时间约8分钟。

Conclusion: DDB证明了通过针对性技术解决方案，交互式调试可以成功扩展到分布式应用。系统在保持低开销的同时，显著提升了调试效率和准确性，为分布式系统开发提供了一种实用的交互式调试方案。

Abstract: Interactive debugging is an effective tool for understanding program behavior at the source level, allowing developers to pause execution, navigate the call stack, and inspect runtime state. However, interactive debuggers are designed for single-process execution, and interactive debugging has been widely considered impractical for distributed systems. Call stacks stop at process boundaries, debugging state fails to survive infrastructure dynamics, and, most critically, debugger-induced execution pauses trigger catastrophic timeout cascades that destroy the intended debug flow. Consequently, developers are forced to abandon live hypothesis testing in favor of unwieldy and iterative log-and-redeploy cycles.
  We present DDB, a source-level interactive debugger that extends interactive debugging capabilities to distributed applications. We show that each of these challenges admits a targeted solution. To bridge disjoint processes, Distributed Backtrace (DBT) embeds compact causality metadata in every RPC and reconstructs a unified call stack across RPC boundaries. To manage the lifecycle of a distributed session, an intent-preserving control plane automatically coordinates and propagates breakpoints across dynamic process sets. To make pausing safe, Pause-Erased Time (PET) virtualizes each process's clock, decoupling logical time from physical pauses and preventing timeout cascades. DDB integrates with an RPC framework in 20-60 lines of code. Evaluated on gRPC, ServiceWeaver, Nu, and Quicksand across up to 122 processes, DDB achieves 30ms median cross-RPC backtrace latency, sub-5 ms time jump under repeated execution pauses, and adds 1-5% throughput overhead, comparable to attaching a single-process debugger. In a controlled user study, DDB achieves a 100% fault localization success rate (compared to 38.5% for baseline tools) with a median localization time of ~8 minutes.

</details>


### [76] [Could Model Partitioning Make Federated Learning More Sustainable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14242)
*Tobias Frohlich,Tiffany Vlaar,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将模型分割技术应用于联邦学习，通过将模型部分卸载至其他参与者来响应低碳或电网信号，可降低高达76%的能耗且无明显开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着联邦学习从低功耗设备扩展到涉及边缘服务器和数据中心的跨孤岛场景，其碳足迹日益受到关注。现有可持续联邦学习方法通过利用低碳能源或减小模型规模来降低能耗，但仍有优化空间。

Method: 提出应用模型分割技术，根据碳感知或电网感知信号，将模型的部分计算卸载给其他参与者，从而转移和降低高碳能源供电客户端的能耗。

Result: 初步结果显示，对于特定的分割点，模型分割可将参与者能耗降低最多76%，且与非分割训练相比无明显的时间或能耗开销。

Conclusion: 模型分割是联邦学习场景下实现可持续计算的有效手段，能够显著降低高碳能耗参与者的能源消耗，同时保证训练效率。

Abstract: As federated learning (FL) extends from distributed machine learning between low-power devices to cross-silo scenarios involving edge servers and data centres, its carbon footprint has become a growing concern. Addressing this, methods for sustainable FL align training with low-carbon energy availability or low grid demand and reduce the energy consumption of clients powered by high-carbon sources by decreasing the size of their models.
  We propose applying model partitioning, which can shift energy consumption by offloading parts of a model to another participant, in response to carbon- or grid-aware signals. Our preliminary findings show that for some partition points, model partitioning can reduce a participant's energy consumption by up to 76% without any significant time or energy consumption overhead compared to non-partitioned training.

</details>


### [77] [Offering Microsecond-Scale Cross-VM Core Elasticity on Colocated Lightweight Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12633)
*Yibo Yan,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了HyperFlux，一种支持弹性并行度调整的超轻量级VM substrate，能在13μs内跨VM迁移物理核心，显著降低高优先级VM的尾延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Serverless平台在VM资源配置上存在两难：为应对突发流量过度配置会降低部署密度，而保持高密度又难以保护尾延迟。当前VM substrate无法实现微秒级物理核心动态调整——传统VM的vCPU热插拔需毫秒级，Firecracker在启动时固定核心数，而最快启动的超轻量VM甚至不支持多核执行。

Method: 提出HyperFlux，一种基于商品级KVM的超轻量VM substrate，实现VM运行时并行度宽度（支撑VM的物理核心数量）的弹性调整。通过优化设计，使物理核心能够以微秒级时间尺度在VM间迁移，包括从繁忙的捐赠者VM强制回收核心。

Result: HyperFlux实现了13μs的跨VM核心迁移速度，比vCPU热插拔快数个数量级；VM内存占用仅3.2MB，冷启动时间1.37ms，与最快启动的超轻量VM相当；在混合部署场景下，与Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor的静态核心共享相比，高负载时高优先级VM尾延迟降低达10倍；相比cgroup和vCPU热插拔，在负载突发变化时能提供更低且更稳定的尾延迟。

Conclusion: HyperFlux首次实现了既支持快速启动和低内存占用，又支持多核并行和微秒级弹性核心调整的超轻量VM substrate，为Serverless平台提供了有效平衡部署密度与尾延迟保护的技术方案。

Abstract: Serverless platforms commonly colocate many diverse workloads, each in a fast-booting, memory-lean virtual machine (VM), to improve deployment density. Overprovisioning each VM for its peak protects tail latency during traffic bursts but hurts density; maintaining high density while effectively protecting tail latency requires the infrastructure to be able to shift physical cores, at a microsecond timescale, to whichever latency-sensitive VM is bursting and reclaim them as the burst subsides. No VM substrate delivers this: conventional VMs resize a guest's cores only through a millisecond-scale vCPU hot-plug path, Firecracker fixes a VM's core count at boot, and the ultralight VMs that boot fastest drop multicore execution entirely.
  We present HyperFlux, a commodity-KVM ultralight VM substrate that makes a VM's parallelism width (the number of physical cores backing it) elastic at runtime. We show that HyperFlux can move a core across VMs in merely 13$μ$s, even when forcibly reclaiming it from a busy donor, orders of magnitude faster than vCPU hot-plug. A HyperFlux VM incurs only a 3.2MB memory footprint and can cold-boot in 1.37ms, on par with the fastest-booting ultralight VMs, while uniquely supporting multicore parallelism. Under colocation, it can reduce high-priority VMs' tail latency by up to 10x under high load compared to static core-sharing with Firecracker and Cloud Hypervisor, and deliver a lower and more stable tail latency compared to using cgroup and vCPU hot-plug under changing load bursts.

</details>


### [78] [Large-scale workflow placement in serverless computing using integer nonlinear programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14427)
*Joshua Adamek,Natalie Carl,Trever Schirmer,Moritz Heinlein,David Bermbach,Sergio Lucia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无服务器边缘计算工作流部署的非线性整数规划模型和分解策略，在降低用户成本的同时缩短工作流执行时间，平均性能提升10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无服务器边缘计算环境中，用户需要在大量异构服务器和边缘设备上部署工作流。现有方法难以在降低用户货币成本的同时最小化工作流执行时间，且面临节点数量扩展性差和节点属性知识分散的挑战。

Method: 1. 建立数学框架对工作流和云节点属性进行建模；2. 将最优放置问题建模为非线性整数规划问题；3. 提出一种新颖的分解策略以解决大规模云/边缘节点扩展及节点属性知识分散的问题。

Result: 案例研究表明，分解策略具有良好的扩展性能；与简单部署启发式方法相比，实现了平均10%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的非线性整数规划模型及分解策略能够有效解决无服务器边缘计算中工作流部署问题，在降低成本和执行时间方面表现出色，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Serverless edge computing has become a powerful cloud framework that enables the execution of large workflows without the need for the user to manage the underlying servers and edge devices. In this work, we address the challenge of deploying these workflows on a large number of different existing servers and edge devices such that monetary costs for the users and workflow evaluation times are minimized. To this end, the workflow and cloud node attributes are modeled in a mathematical framework. As a result, we present a novel model of the optimal placement problem as a nonlinear integer program. To solve both the issues of scaling towards a larger number of cloud/edge nodes as well as decomposed knowledge of node attributes, we propose a novel decomposition strategy. In a case study, we show the beneficial scaling properties of the decomposition approach and a mean improvement of 10% against a simple deployment heuristic.

</details>


### [79] [Validating LLM-Modernized Scientific Software Through Differential Fault Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14527)
*Evan Coleman,Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种差分故障注入验证方法，用于验证LLM对Fortran科学软件的现代化改造是否保留了原始代码在故障、扰动和精度降低情况下的行为一致性，实验表明原始代码和现代化代码在所有测试中均保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的Fortran科学软件现代化改造验证主要关注正常执行情况，忽略了对故障、扰动和精度降低等异常情况响应能力的验证，可能导致现代化后的代码在可靠性方面与原始代码存在差异。

Method: 提出差分故障注入验证方法：通过测试工具在GAMESS的共享自洽场驱动程序的12个站点进行插桩，对原始Fortran实现和LLM现代化实现施加相同的确定性故障，从而隔离并测试转换后的积分内核。

Result: 在超过2200次运行中，瞬态故障吸收成本与收缩模型匹配（预测斜率0.74和1.49次迭代/比特，实测0.82和1.50）；持续扰动使每增加一个比特的最终能量误差减半；发现了阶段相关的并行死锁和精度降低导致的错误收敛问题。原始内核与现代化内核在所有200次配对注入中完全一致，测量引导的同步变更与现代化改造组合后在所有40次配对中也完全匹配。

Conclusion: 差分故障注入方法能够有效验证LLM现代化改造是否保留了原始代码的故障响应特性，为科学软件现代化提供了可靠的验证手段，确保现代化后的代码在可靠性方面与原始代码保持一致。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to modernize the legacy Fortran underlying production scientific software, but validation of these transformations emphasizes nominal executions and may not test whether a modernization preserves the original code's response to faults, perturbations, and reduced precision. We present a differential fault-injection validation method: a harness instruments the shared self-consistent-field driver of GAMESS at twelve sites and applies identical, deterministic faults to the original and LLM-modernized implementations, isolating the converted integral kernels. Across more than 2,200 runs, transient-fault absorption costs match a contraction-based model (predicted slopes 0.74 and 1.49 iterations per bit; measured 0.82 and 1.50), persistent perturbations halve final-energy error per additional bit, and the campaigns expose phase-dependent parallel deadlocks and false convergence under reduced precision. The original and modernized kernels agree in all 200 paired injections, and a measurement-guided synchronization change composes with the modernization, matching in all 40 pairs.

</details>

### [80] [Robust Constraint-Aware Bayesian Tuning of BBRv2 for QUIC under Tactile Internet Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14318)
*Muhammad Hanif Lashari,Shakil Ahmed,Wafa Batayneh,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于贝叶斯优化的BBRv2参数调优框架，在QUIC协议中实现了面向触觉互联网应用的性能优化，有效改善了尾延迟、抖动和丢包性能，同时保持了吞吐量竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉互联网应用对延迟、抖动、丢包和响应性有严格要求，虽然BBRv2提供了基于模型的拥塞控制框架并具有强大的吞吐潜力，但其默认行为可能无法很好地适应延迟敏感的交互场景。因此需要针对触觉互联网需求对BBRv2进行参数调优。

Method: 提出一个鲁棒的约束感知调优框架，将参数选择建模为多模拟网络条件下的昂贵黑盒优化问题。采用贝叶斯优化方法配合树形Parzen估计器(TPE)高效探索有界参数空间。优化目标设计为在限制尾延迟和丢包的同时保持吞吐量，并通过抖动指标独立评估延迟不稳定性。

Result: 在低、中、高网络损伤场景下的实验结果表明，调优后的配置相比标准QUIC拥塞控制基线，在保持竞争性吞吐量的同时，显著改善了尾延迟、抖动行为和丢包性能。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的黑盒调优是一种将QUIC传输行为适配到触觉互联网风格要求的实用方法，证明了自动参数优化在满足严格性能约束方面的有效性。

Abstract: Tactile Internet applications place strict require- ments on latency, jitter, loss, and responsiveness, which makes transport configuration a critical design factor. Although BBRv2 offers a model-based congestion control framework with strong throughput potential, its default behavior may not be well aligned with delay-sensitive interactive scenarios. This paper presents a robust and constraint-aware tuning framework for BBRv2 in QUIC, where parameter selection is formulated as an expensive black-box optimization problem over multiple emulated network conditions. The tuning process uses Bayesian optimization with the Tree Structured Parzen Estimator to efficiently explore a bounded parameter space under noisy experimental measure- ments. The objective is designed to preserve throughput while enforcing limits on tail latency and loss, while delay instability is evaluated separately through the jitter metric. Experimental results across low, medium, and high impairment scenarios show that the tuned configuration improves tail latency, jitter behavior, and loss performance while maintaining competitive goodput relative to standard QUIC congestion control baselines. These results support robust black-box tuning as a practical method for adapting QUIC transport behavior to tactile Internet style requirements.

</details>


### [81] [Scaling 5G-TSN Bridges: Operating Regimes, Scheduling, and Time Synchronisation Under Heterogeneous Industrial Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14386)
*Mohamed Seliem,Utz Roedig,Cormac Sreenan,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过仿真评估了5G NR基站作为TSN透明网桥时的可扩展性，发现QoS感知调度器在饱和状态下能显著降低关键流延迟，并提出了优化时钟同步和降低延迟的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3GPP Release 16虽然实现了5G作为IEEE 802.1 TSN透明网桥的功能，但在异构工业负载下的可扩展性尚未得到充分表征，缺乏对单小区能支持的TSN端点数量及其QoS性能退化的定量研究。

Method: 基于OMNeT++/Simu5G平台使用nascTime框架进行仿真，建模闭环控制、机器视觉、批量遥测和IEEE 802.1AS四种流量，采用四承载SDAP架构，变化参数包括端点数量(1-40)、MAC调度器类型、无线带宽(10/20 MHz)和信道模型。

Result: 发现三个操作区域：饱和前各调度器性能相近；接近饱和时QoS感知PF调度器相比信道感知和公平调度器将关键流P99延迟降低两个数量级；过载时仅QoS-PF能维持最高优先级流量的近完全交付。带宽翻倍使饱和阈值约翻倍。专用高优先级承载承载IEEE 802.1AS/gPTP流量可减少时钟伺服不稳定性，但饱和时低优先级端点仍经历同步延迟升高。Sub-6 GHz/30 kHz-SCS配置的有效延迟底限约2.25 ms。

Conclusion: 5G-TSN网桥的QoS性能强烈依赖于调度器选择和操作区域，QoS感知调度对维持工业级性能至关重要；sub-6 GHz配置难以满足sub-3 ms时延要求，需采用配置授权或更高数值学等无线配置变更来突破延迟底限。

Abstract: 3GPP Release 16 enables a 5G system to operate as a transparent IEEE 802.1 TSN bridge, but its scalability under heterogeneous industrial workloads remains insufficiently characterised. This paper uses the nascTime framework on OMNeT++/Simu5G to evaluate how many TSN endpoints a single 5G NR cell can bridge before per-flow QoS degrades. We model closed-loop control, machine vision, bulk telemetry, and IEEE 802.1AS traffic over a four-bearer SDAP architec- ture, varying the number of endpoints from 1 to 40, MAC scheduler, radio bandwidth (10 MHz and 20 MHz), and channel model. Results show three operating regimes. Below saturation, non-DRR schedulers perform similarly; near saturation, QoS- aware PF reduces critical-flow P99 latency by up to two or- ders of magnitude relative to channel-aware and fairness-based schedulers; and under overload, only QoS-PF maintains near- complete delivery for the highest-priority traffic. Across the two evaluated bandwidths, the saturation threshold approximately doubles when bandwidth doubles. We also show that isolating IEEE 802.1AS/gPTP traffic on a dedicated high-priority radio bearer reduces clock-servo instability, although endpoints carry- ing lower-priority data still experience elevated synchronisation delay under saturation because of reduced MAC scheduling frequency.
